# Reconciled Investigative Public-Record API Catalogue — Volume 3 of 5

Systems SYS-0566 through SYS-0926. This is a companion source to `investigative_api_catalogue_00_guide.txt`. Each system record remains intact with its access points, coverage mappings, ranking, and verification material. Stable identifiers should be used when joining records across volumes.

## SYS-0566 — Ghana SEC unlicensed-entity notices

- **Publisher:** Securities and Exchange Commission Ghana
- **Official domain:** sec.gov.gh
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Ghana
- **Coarse record family:** Sanctions, regulation & enforcement
- **Official or non-official status:** Official / public authority
- **Geographic coverage:** Named entities that the regulator says are not licensed to conduct capital-market activity.
- **Access modes:** Browse / download
- **Representative URL:** https://sec.gov.gh/public-notice-list-of-entities-operating-without-a-license/
- **Source-system confidence:** Medium
